Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the seismology space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 453 and the convergence rate is O(800/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 453 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the seismology structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 800. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
